Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any kinetics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 125. The projective resolution has length at most 72 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the kinetics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 125 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 72. QED.
